Construct sparse coefficients efficiently for active lags:

Game approximation for g:
Function `fw_direction_game(f, time_limit, active_mask, iterations=200, eta=1.0)`:

- For each t:
   Compute pressure p_i = ∑_λ A_{k,i}. This is convolution of λ_full (zeros outside active) with f as derived but with factor? A_{k,i}=f_{k-i}; no factor 2. We can compute p = gradient_conv(lambda_full, f, factor=1): `conv(f, lambda_rev)[n-1:2n-1][::-1]`. (No 2). Need length n.
   Normalize? Values scale with λ sum1, f sum1, p_i ~1/n.
   Determine g as distribution over coordinates. The subproblem optimum g puts mass on minimum pressure. To regularize, use softmax with high temperature: temp = (min_pressure + 0.01*(max-min)?). But we need efficient. Choose lowest `n_low` (e.g., 64 or 128) pressure indices; set g uniform over them. Or use `g = 1/(pressure - min + eps)` normalized over all? n=6700, direct O(n), okay.    If g too concentrated, update may be poor. Use mix: g = (1-α)*e_min + α*inverse_pressure over low set? Could set `g = 1/(pressure - min + sigma)` with sigma adapt; normalized. This produces smooth direction.
   Update λ: We need subgradient A[:,i] for chosen g distribution: gradλ_k = (f*g)_k over active. Compute h=f*g full via FFT (length 2n-1). Then λ = softmax(η * (h_active - baseline) + log λ). But h values small ~1/n; η large. Use log update: logλ += η*(h_active - h_active.mean()) to keep scale; then clip and normalize.
   Track approximate value: m_t = np.min(pressure) (lower bound? For any λ, min pressure ≤ dual value? Actually dual objective min_i A^Tλ; maximizing over λ. Track max over t of min pressure). The primal optimum t* = dual value. If t* < c_max(f) (M_curr), direction reduces. We can just use g.
- After iterations, return g simplex.

FW update line search:
Given f_old, g from subproblem. We can try mixtures α in [small...1]. New a = normalize((1-α)f + α g), require nonnegative (already). Compute actual proxy max (full c) and choose best α. Since c is quadratic, a line search over α can be approximated: c(α)= (1-α)^2 c_f + 2α(1-α) f*g + α^2 c_g. We can compute c_g? Might not. Simpler sample α: [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5], take best after FFT. For time, maybe 5 evaluations per FW iter. If many iters, heavy. But we can do only when nice.

But the LP/FW direction may not improve max if active set incomplete. We can after each update compute full c, active top K, add violated lags where f*g > max(c) etc.

Could implement `fw_loop_game(a0,time_limit, active_count, mw_iters, ...)`:
- a = norm_sum1(a0); c=fft_conv(a); M=c.max(); update best.
- active_idx = top_lags where c > M - tol (e.g., top active_count or margin). Use sorted c.
- Iterate until time:
   g = game_direction(f=a, active_idx, mw_iters=50/100, low_count=64)
   # line search few α: 0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5, maybe 0.05
   best trial a and M among α; also check actual evaluate? Use proxy.

- Return best.

Functions:

`next_pow2(x)`:
```python
def next_pow2(x):
    return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()
```

`fft_conv(a)`:
```python
def fft_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(a)
    size=2*n-1
    N = 1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)[:size]
    c = np.where(c > 0, c, 0.0)
    return c
```
Maybe due numerical c edges negative; set 0.

`fft_cross_pressure(p, a)`: p length 2n-1 (weights for c). Need compute grad_a (without factor maybe 1 or 2). Use general formula: `conv_api = irfft(fft(a,Nr) * fft(p_rev,Nr), Nr)[:?]`, where size_conv = n + len(p) -1 = 3n-2. N = next_pow2(3n-2). `conv = irfft(A*P, N)[:3*n-2]`; `out = 2.0 * conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]`. Need ensure indexing length: 2n-1 slice length n? n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n. Reverse.
Check with n=1: p length1, conv length1, slice n-1=0 to 1 length1, reverse ok; grad=2 p0 a0. c0=a0^2 derivative 2a0. good.
For p zeros, ok.

But if p is symmetric and we can use length 2n-1; N for 3n-2 ~32768. Could precompute? Not across lengths.

`lse_objective_gradient_a(a, beta, mask=None, reg=0.0)` returns f, grad_a. Use for simplex gradient perhaps.
```python
def lse_a(a,beta,mask=None,reg=0.0):
  c=fft_conv(a)
  if mask is None: cv=c
  else: cv=c[mask]
  m=cv.max()
  sh=beta*(cv-m)
  sh=np.clip(sh,-700,700)
  e=np.exp(sh)
  p=e/e.sum()
  pf=np.zeros_like(c)
  if mask is None: pf[:]=p
  else: pf[mask]=p
  grad = 2*conv_pressure(pf,a)
  f = m + np.log(e.sum())/beta
  if reg>0:
     f += 0.5*reg*np.dot(a,a)
     grad += reg*a
  return f, grad
```
But `e.sum()` after clip can underflow all except max; ok at least one 1. If beta negative? no.

`softmax_from_u(u)`:
```python
def softmax_from_u(u):
  u=max
  e=np.exp(u-max)
  s=e.sum()
  if s<=0 or not finite: return uniform
  return e/s
```

`objgrad_u(u,beta,mask=None,reg=0.0)`:
```python
a=softmax_from_u(u)
f,grad_a=lse_a(a,beta,mask,reg)
mean=np.dot(a,grad_a)
gu=a*(grad_a-mean)
return f,gu
```
Need top-level no closure.

For scipy minimize, `objgrad_u` with `args=(beta, mask, reg)`. If mask None or np array. Passing large array each time? args stored, okay.

Safe eval:
```python
def safe_eval(a):
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
       arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
       if arr.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)) or arr.sum()<0.01: return inf
       # ensure list floats finite no nan
       lst=[float(x) for x in arr]
       val=evaluate_sequence(lst)
       if val is None or not np.isfinite(val): return inf
       return float(val)
    except: return inf
```
Will evaluate_sequence perhaps expect list not np. Good.

def run_lbfegs(a0, budget, beta, mask, reg, maxfun, maxiter):
    n=len(a0)
    a=norm_sum1(a0)
    u=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    u-=u.mean()
    res=scipy.optimize.minimize(objgrad_u, u, args=(beta, mask, reg), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxfun':maxfun,'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-7})
    a_out=softmax_from_u(res.x)
    return a_out, res
```

But the MW update for max-min: We have dual λ maximizing φ(λ)=min_i pressure_i. Subgradient is row for i* (or average over selected low coordinates g). We want mirror descent (gradient ascent): λ_{new} ∝ λ * exp(η * (A[:,i*] - baseline)). For randomized g, subgradient E[A^T?]. A[:,i] = f_{?}; h_active = ∑_i g_i A_{k,i} = f*g_k. Good. def coordinate_polish(a0,budget):
 a=norm; c=fft_conv; M=max; best=a,M; stall=0
 t0=time
 rng
 while time<budget and stall<100:
    top_idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:min(20,len(c))]
    p_top=zeros(size); p_top[top_idx]=1/len(top_idx)
    pressure=cross_convolve_p_a(p_top,a,2.0)  # gradient for avg
    # candidate sources: top 8 by a*pressure
    src_scores = a*pressure
    top_src = argpart(src_scores,8)[:8]
    # destination: low pressure, perhaps not in top contribution? use pressure + small*a
    dst_scores = pressure + 1e-6*a
    low_dst = argpartition(dst_scores,12)[:12]
    candidates=[]
    for s in top_src:
       if a[s]<=1e-12: continue
       for d in low_dst:
          if d==s: continue
          for frac in [0.01,0.02,0.05]:
             candidates...
    # evaluate top candidates by approximate delta: compute delta_maybe? Simpler use vector rank update? Maybe do FFT for up to 50 best approx.

Cross gradient formula test with n=2. a=[a0,a1], c=[a0^2,2a0a1,a1^2]. p=[p0,p1,p2]. Desired grad:
grad0=2(p0 a0 + p1 a1)
grad1=2(p1 a0 + p2 a1)

Now L-BFGS gradient in softmax:
`val = LSE(a)`. `g_a=∇_a LSE`. `g_u_j = ∑_{i} g_a_i da_i/du_j = a_j(g_a_j - ∑_m a_m g_a_m)`. This is correct for softmax. Add reg_u: derivative wrt u of 0.5mu||u||^2 = mu u. Reg_a: derivative wrt a of 0.5 reg||a||^2 = reg a; then to u as above.

Scipy L-BFGS-B may call with `u` having large mean; reg_u prevents drift. But objective also has invariance; reg_u creates optimum near zero mean. Good.

Potential issue: The LSE objective is not strongly convex; L-BFGS may take too large steps resulting in some a_i underflow, `log(a)` for next initialization. To continue, after run we compute a=softmax(res.x); if min(a) < 1e-300, log for next will have -inf. We can instead keep `res.x` centered and use directly for next u (softmax of res.x) rather than log(a). But res.x may already represent. Since softmax invariant, using res.x is fine. For final evaluate, use softmax(res.x). For next beta, use u0=res.x centered (subtract mean). This avoids log underflow. We'll return both.

`run_lbfegs` should return `a_out, u_out` where u_out=res.x centered. If res fails, maybe use u0.

Now details of `run_lbfegs`:
```python
def run_lbfegs(a0, budget, beta, mask, reg_a, reg_u, maxfun, maxiter, verbose):
    if scipy is None: return a0, np.log(...)
    a=norm_sum1(a0); n=len(a)
    u=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); u-=u.mean()
    t0=time.monotonic()
    options={'maxfun':maxfun,'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-7,'disp':False}
    try:
       res=scipy.optimize.minimize(objgrad_u, u, args=(beta, mask, reg_a, reg_u), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=options)
    except Exception as e:
       print('L-BFGS failed', e); res=None
    if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
       u_out=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
       a_out=softmax_from_u(u_out)
       # center u_out before return
       u_out=u_out-u_out.mean()
    else:
       a_out=a; u_out=u
    if verbose: print run time, res fun if available
    return a_out,u_out
```
Need `softmax_from_u` top-level.

`objgrad_u` must handle `mask` None or bool array. If mask length but selection size 0? return uniform grad zero. If `u` contains NaN? Return inf.
```python
def objgrad_u(u,beta,mask=None,reg_a=0.0,reg_u=1e-8):
  u=np.asarray(u,dtype=float)
  if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)): return inf, zeros
  a=softmax_from_u(u)
  f,ga=lse_a(a,beta,mask,reg_a)
  if not finite f: f=1e100
  dot=np.dot(a,ga)
  gu=a*(ga-dot)
  if reg_u>0: f+=0.5*reg_u*np.dot(u,u); gu=gu+reg_u*u
  if not finite gu: gu=zeros
  return f,gu
```

`lse_a` needs if mask bool and number selected K maybe 1; LSE = cv[0]. Gradient p=1 at that index. Good.
Potential issue: `c[mask]` returns float array; if mask None, `cv=c`. For `p_full`, if mask is None, assigning p (same length) ok. If mask bool, `p_full[mask]=p` works if p length count. Good.
But if mask is an integer array? We'll use bool or None.

`fft_conv`: For very large n (e.g., 10000), N=32768 for 2n? Actually 2n-1=19999, N=32768. Good. For pressure N=nextpow2(3n-2) maybe 32768 for n=6700, for n=10000 32768? 3n=30000 ->32768; good. For n=20000 ->262144, slower. We avoid huge.

def game_direction(f, active_idx, mw_iters=30, temp_factor=0.05, seed=0):
    f=norm_sum1(f); n=len(f); size=2*n-1

    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # initialize lambda with random or uniform? Could try multiple initializations? Use c values? We don't pass current c. Pass optional init_logits.
```

```
    for it in range(mw_iters):
        pf=np.zeros(size)
        pf[active_idx]=np.exp(logits-logits.max()); normalize
        pressure=cross_convolve_p_a(pf,f,1.0)
        pressure=np.maximum(pressure,0)
        ptp=pressure.max()-pressure.min()
        if ptp < 1e-30:
            g=np.ones(n)/n
        else:
            temp=temp_factor*ptp
            w=np.exp(-10.0*(pressure-pressure.min())/(10.0*temp)? Wait if temp_factor=0.05, we want scaled by ptp. Use `scaled=(pressure-minp)/ptp; w=np.exp(-15.0*scaled)`; gives max e-15 small, min1. Support ~1/15 of ptp. Good.
            w=w + 1e-12
            g=w/w.sum()
            # mix small uniform
            g = 0.98*g + 0.02*np.ones(n)/n
            g /= g.sum()
        # compute h
        h=fft_conv2(f,g)
        ha=h[active_idx]
        ha=np.maximum(ha,0)
        ptp_h=ha.max()-ha.min()
        if ptp_h < 1e-30:
            logits += 0
        else:
            eta=20.0/ptp_h
            logits += eta*(ha - ha.mean())
        logits -= logits.max()
    # final g from last pressure? Need recompute after final logits.
    pf... pressure... g...
    return g
```

`lp_direction_hihts` if HiGHS unavailable. We can test once:
```
def hihts_available():
  global _FLAG
  if _FLAG is None:
     try:
       x=linprog([-1], bounds=[(0,None)], method='HiGHS', options={'disp':False})
       _FLAG = x.success
     except: _FLAG=False
  return _FLAG

`line_search_mix` with alphas:
```
def line_search_mix(f,g,cf,cg,fg,mask,best_M,t=1):
    alphas=[max(0.01,1.0/(t+1)), max(0.02,2.0/(t+2)), 0.05,0.1,0.15,0.25,0.4,0.6,0.8,1.0]
    best_alpha=0; best_m=best_M
    for a in alphas:
       if a<=0 or a>1: continue
       val=(1-a)**2*cf + 2*a*(1-a)*fg + a*a*cg
       # clip small negative
       if mask is None: m=val.max()
       else: m=val[mask].max()
       if m<best_m: best_m=m; best_alpha=a
    if best_alpha==0:
       return f.copy(), best_M, 0
    new=(1-best_alpha)*f + best_alpha*g
    new=np.maximum(new,0); s=new.sum(); new/=s
    return new,best_m,best_alpha
```
Need if `best_M` maybe proxy max of current f; if new mixture equals f at α=0. Good.

However if `g` from LP exact minimizes active max but not full, line search might have no α <1 improving. We can try α=1= g? Might reduce. If no, no improve.

def coordinate_polish(a0,budget,mask,verbose=True):
   t0=time.monotonic(); time_limit=budget
   a=norm_sum1(a0); c=fft_conv(a); M=proxy_max(c,mask); best_a=a; best_M=M
   size=2*n-1; arange=np.arange(n)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(888)
   stall=0
   round=0
   while time_left>2 and stall<80:
      round+=1
      top_kနေ = min(40,size)
      top_idx=active_top(c,mask,top_k)
      if len(top_idx)==0: break
      p_top=np.zeros(size); p_top[top_idx]=1.0/len(top_idx)
      pressure=cross_convolve_p_a(p_top,a,2.0)  # gradient avg top
      np.maximum(pressure,0,out=pressure)
      # source candidates: high pressure * a; but also consider top peak specific.
      src_score = pressure * (0.5 + a)  #
      src_count = min(8,n)
      top_src=np.argpartition(src_score, src_count)[:src_count]
      dst_score = pressure + 1e-6*a
      dst_count = min(12,n)
      low_dst=np.argpartition(dst_score, dst_count)[:dst_count]

          if s==d: continue
          src=int(s); dst=int(d)

             if tmove <= 1e-15: continue

         all_cand.sort(key=lambda? no lambda allowed. Can't use lambda. Use `all_cand.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])` prohibited? Rule says no lambda functions. ```
def apply_single_move(c,a,s,d,t):
   c_new=c.copy()
   c_new[d:d+n] += 2*t*a
   c_new[s:s+n] -= 2*t*a
   size=len(c)
   if 2*d < size: c_new[2*d] += t*t
   if 2*s < size: c_new[2*s] += t*t
   if s+d < size: c_new[s+d] -= 2*t*t
   c_new=np.maximum(c_new,0)
   return c_new
```
But when we call for many candidates, this allocates. Could directly max to reduce memory? Allocating 100*13399*8=100MB over time, okay. Could compute change and update a temporary vector? Fine.

```
def softmax_from_u(u):
   u=np.asarray(u,float)
   if not finite: return uniform
   m=u.max()
   e=np.exp(u-m)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: uniform
   return e/s
```

def active_top(c,mask,maxK):
  size=len(c)
  maxK=min(maxK,size)
  if maxK<=0: return np.array([],int)
  if mask is None:
     if maxK>=size: return np.arange(size)
     idx=np.argpartition(c, size-maxK)[::-1]  # top maxK unsorted? Actually partition at size-maxK gives largest in first? Let's use argsort simple:
     return np.argsort(c)[::-1][:maxK]
  else:
     vals=c.copy(); vals[~mask]= -np.inf
     return np.argsort(vals)[::-1][:maxK]
```

`objgrad_v(v,beta,mask,reg_a,reg_u,ref)`:
```
n=len(v)+1; u=np.empty(n); ... u[ref]=0
a=softmax(u) # but u already relative, softmax equals normalized ratios
f,ga=lse_a(a,beta,mask,reg_a)
dot=np.dot(a,ga)
gu=a*(ga-dot)
gv=np.empty_like(v); gv[:ref]=gu[:ref]; gv[ref:]=gu[ref+1:]
if reg_u>0: f+=0.5*reg_u*np.dot(v,v); gv+=reg_u*v
return f,gv
```
No need center.

Scipy on v dimension n-1. The objective no longer invariant; reg_u can be 0 or small. Use reg_u=0 maybe. Flat direction removed exactly. Good.

Global flags for HIGHS:
```
_HI_GH_AVAILABLE = None
def hihts_available():
    global _HI_GH_AVAILABLE
    if _HI_GH_AVAILABLE is None:
       ok=False
       if SCIPY_OK:
          try:
             res=scipy.optimize.linprog([-1.0], bounds=[(0.0,None)], method='HiGHS', options={'disp': False})
             ok=bool(res.success)
          except Exception: ok=False
       _HI_GH_AVAILABLE=ok
    return _HI_GH_AVAILABLE
```
If HiGHS not available, linprog method='HiGHS' may raise; catch.

`safe_eval`: Need not import evaluate_sequence; assume global. If evaluate_sequence not defined? It will be. Catch NameError? In code, calling undefined raises. We can wrap.
```
def safe_eval(a):
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
       if arr.size==0: return inf
       arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return inf
       s=arr.sum()
       if s<0.01: return inf
       lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
       val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
       if val is None: return inf
       val=float(val)
       if not math.isfinite(val): return inf
       return val
    except Exception:
       return inf
```
If evaluator clamps to [0,1000], our arr already. Good.

`try_update`:
```
def try_update(best_seq,best_score,a,label,t_global):
    if a is None: return best_seq,best_score
    try:
       arr=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64),0.0)
       s=arr.sum()
       if s<0.01 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return best_seq,best_score
       arr=arr/s
       sc=safe_eval(arr)
       if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
          best_seq=arr.copy(); best_score=float(sc)
          print("NEWBEST " + label + " score=" + repr(sc) + " n=" + str(len(arr)))
    except Exception:
       pass
    return best_seq,best_score
```
Could if sc close but not lower, not update. Good.

`norm_sum1`: Use floor max 0.
```
def norm_sum1(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    arr[~np.isfinite(arr)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(arr,0.0,out=arr)
    s=arr.sum()
    n=arr.size
    if s<1e-15 or n==0:
       return np.ones(n)/n if n>0 else np.ones(1)
    return arr/s
```

`proxy_score_from_coeff`: maybe `2*n*M`.

def choose_mask(a, actual_score):
    n=len(a); c=fft_conv(a); size=2*n-1

       m_no_center=m.copy(); m_no_center[n-1]=False
       m_no_edges=m.copy(); m_no_edges[0]=False; m_no_edges[-1]=False
       m_no_center_edges=m_no_center.copy(); m_no_center_edges[0]=False; m_no_center_edges[-1]=False
       m_left=np.zeros(size,bool); m_left[:n]=True

    return best_mask
```

`make_v`: Need if a size 1 return empty. If ref max but a[ref] very small due all zero? norm_sum1 ensures. Use `a_ref=max(float(a[ref]),1e-300)`.
```
def make_v(a, ref=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=a.size
   if n==1: return np.array([],float), 0
   if ref is None:
       if a[0]>0: ref=int(np.argmax(a)) else ref=0
   ref=int(ref)
   a_ref=max(float(a[ref]),1e-300)
   log=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)) - np.log(a_ref)
   if ref==0: v=log[1:]
   elif ref==n-1: v=log[:-1]
   else: v=np.concatenate((log[:ref], log[ref+1:]))
   return v, ref
```
If a has zeros, log 1e-300 - log a_ref. If a_ref large? ok.

`from_v`:
```
def from_v(v, ref):
   n=len(v)+1
   u=np.zeros(n,float)
   v=np.asarray(v,float)
   if ref>0: u[:ref]=v[:ref]
   if ref<n-1: u[ref+1:]=v[ref:]
   return softmax_from_u(u)
```

`lse_a`: Need if mask is boolean with selected size K. If K==0, f=0, grad zeros. Objective LSE with no terms 0. But not used.
```
def lse_a(a,beta,mask=None,reg_a=0.0):
   n=len(a); size=2*n-1
   N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:size]
   c=np.where(c>0.0,c,0.0)
   if mask is None:
      cv=c
   else:
      cv=c[mask]
   K=cv.size
   if K==0: f=0; grad=np.zeros(n)
   else:
      m=float(np.max(cv))
      sh=beta*(cv-m)
      sh=np.clip(sh,-700,700)
      e=np.exp(sh)
      se=e.sum()
      if se<=0 or not finite: e=np.zeros(K); e[0]=1; se=1
      p=e/se
      pf=np.zeros(size,float)
      if mask is None: pf[:]=p
      else: pf[mask]=p
      Pr=np.fft.rfft(pf[::-1],N)
      conv=np.fft.irfft(A*Pr,N)[:3*n-2]
      grad=2.0*conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
      f=m + math.log(float(se))/beta
   np.maximum(grad, -? no, gradient can negative)
   if reg_a>0: f += 0.5*reg_a*float(np.dot(a,a)); grad = grad + reg_a*a
   return f,grad
```
Need if N=1? n at least1, 3n-2=1, next_pow2(1)=? Our next_pow2: `1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()`. For x=1, (0).bit_length()=0, 1<<0=1. Good.
`np.fft.rfft` with n=1 works.
Gradient may have tiny imaginary due irfft; it returns real.

But if mask not None and mask is integer array, `pf[mask]=p`; boolean expected. We'll ensure.

`objgrad_v`:
```
def objgrad_v(v,beta,mask=None,reg_a=0.0,reg_u=0.0,ref=0):
   v=np.asarray(v,float)
   n=len(v)+1
   if v.ndim !=1 or len(v)!=n-1: try fix? return inf zeros
   u=np.empty(n,float); u[ref]=0
   if ref>0: u[:ref]=v[:ref]
   if ref<n-1: u[ref+1:]=v[ref:]
   a=softmax_from_u(u)
   f,ga=lse_a(a,beta,mask,reg_a)
   if not math.isfinite(f): f=1e100
   mean=float(np.dot(a,ga))
   gu=a*(ga-mean)
   gv=np.empty(n-1,float)
   if ref>0: gv[:ref]=gu[:ref]
   if ref<n-1: gv[ref:]=gu[ref+1:]
   if reg_u>0:
      f += 0.5*reg_u*float(np.dot(v,v))
      gv = gv + reg_u*v
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(gv)): gv=np.zeros_like(gv)
   return f,gv
```
Potential: If ref >= n? check.

`run_lbfegs`:
```
def run_lbfegs(a0,budget,beta,mask,reg_a=0.0,reg_u=0.0,maxfun=500,maxiter=500,verbose=True):
   a=norm_sum1(a0); n=a.size
   if n==1: return a, np.array([]), 0
   v0,ref=make_v(a)
   t0=time.monotonic()
   if not SCIPY_OK or budget <=0 or maxfun<=0:
      return a, v0, ref
   options={'maxfun': int(maxfun), 'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-7, 'disp':False}
   try:
      res=scipy.optimize.minimize(objgrad_v, v0, args=(beta,mask,reg_a,reg_u,ref), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=options)
   except Exception as e:
      print("L-BFGS exception: " + repr(e))
      res=None
   if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(np.asarray(res.x))) and hasattr(res,'success'):
       vx=np.asarray(res.x,float)
       a_out=from_v(vx,ref)
       a_out=norm_sum1(a_out)
       v_out,ref_out=make_v(a_out)
       if verbose:
          print("L-BFGS beta=" + repr(beta) + " n=" + str(n) + " time=" + str(time.monotonic()-t0) + " f=" + repr(getattr(res,'fun',None)) + " success=" + str(getattr(res,'success',False)))
       return a_out,v_out,ref_out
   else:
       if verbose: print("L-BFGS failed, use start")
       return a, v0, ref
```
If res not success but x maybe improved; still use if finite. If not, return start. Could use last x even if not success. Good.

Need cost estimate function:
```
def estimate_lbfegs_cost(a,mask,beta=30):
   if not SCIPY_OK: return 1.0
   v,ref=make_v(a)
   t0=time.monotonic()
   try:
      f,g=objgrad_v(v,beta,mask,0,0,ref)
      cost=time.monotonic()-t0
   except: cost=0.1
   if cost<=0: cost=0.02
   return float(cost)
```

def lp_direction_hihts(f, active_idx, time_limit=10.0, maxK=1000):
   if not hihts_available(): return None
   f=norm_sum1(f); n=f.size
   idx=np.asarray(active_idx,int)
   if idx.size==0: return None
   if idx.size > maxK: idx=idx[:maxK]  # assumes sorted descending. If not, maybe sort by active values? No. Could return only top from active_top sorted, okay.
   K=idx.size
   # build sparse
   ...
   c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[n]=1.0
   A_ub=sparse.hstack((A, sparse.csc_matrix(-np.ones((K,1)))), format='csr')
   A_eq=np.ones((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,n]=0.0
   b_eq=np.ones(1)
   bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n + [(None, None)]
   try:
      res=scipy.optimize.linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=np.zeros(K), A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='HiGHS', options={'disp':False, 'time_limit': float(time_limit)})
   except Exception:
      try without time_limit
   if res is not None and res.success:
      g=np.asarray(res.x[:n],float); g=np.maximum(g,0); gs=g.sum(); if gs>1e-15 return g/gs
   return None
```

```
def random_transfer_improve(a,c,M,mask,rand_count,rng):
 for _ in range(rand_count):
   s=int(rng.integers(n)); d=int(rng.integers(n)); if s==d or a[s]<1e-12: continue
   tfloat=a[s]*float(rng.uniform(0.001,0.05)); tfloat=min(tfloat,a[s]*0.99)
   c_new=apply_single_move(c,a,s,d,tfloat)
   mn=proxy_max(c_new,mask)
   if mn < M - 1e-17:
       a[s]-=tfloat; a[d]+=tfloat; a=np.maximum(a,0); ssum=a.sum(); a/=ssum
       c=fft_conv(a); M=mn? recompute M=proxy_max(c,mask)
       return a,c,M,True
 return a,c,M,False
```

          try_update
    # final validation

    if math.isfinite(final_val):
       # maybe if best_score > final_val update
    else: fallback
    return [float(x) for x in np.clip(seq_best,0,1000)]

def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()
```

```
def fft_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    size=2*n-1
    N=next_pow2(size)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:size]
    c[np.isfinite(c)&(c>0)]? Use `c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)`. This will convert -nan? If c nan, comparison false? np.where(nan>0 false ->0). Good.
    return c
```

```python
"""
Hybrid local search for non-negative sequences minimizing
2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The objective is hill-shaped in the space of probability vectors, so the
program combines:
1. L-BFGS on log-sum-exp smoothed objectives in a softmax reference coordinate
   system, using both full peak sets and active peak sets.
2. Frank-Wolfe directions. The convex subproblem min_{g simplex} max_k f*g
   is solved approximately as a zero-sum game, and occasionally solved exactly
   with HiGHS when active constraints are limited.
3. Exact single-coordinate transfer polish, using the rank-1 update of the
   convolution to test many small moves rapidly.
4. Length probes and final polishing.

The final returned sequence is always non-negative and normalized.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    import scipy.optimize
    import scipy.sparse
    SCIPY_OK = True
except Exception:
    SCIPY_OK = False

_HI_GHS_AVAILABLE = None


def hihts_available():
    global _HI_GHS_AVAILABLE
    if _HI_GHS_AVAILABLE is None:
        ok = False
        if SCIPY_OK:
            try:
                res = scipy.optimize.linprog([-1.0], bounds=[(0.0, None)],
                                             method="HiGHS", options={"disp": False})
                ok = bool(res.success)
            except Exception:
                ok = False
        _HI_GHS_AVAILABLE = ok
    return _HI_GHS_AVAILABLE


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def norm_sum1(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    bad = ~np.isfinite(arr)
    if np.any(bad):
        arr[bad] = 0.0
    np.maximum(arr, 0.0, out=arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return arr / s


def fft_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:size]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c) & (c > 0.0), c, 0.0)
    return c


def fft_conv2(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size != n:
        b = np.resize(b, n)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, N)[:size]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c) & (c > 0.0), c, 0.0)
    return c


def cross_convolve_p_a(p, a, factor=1.0):
    """
    Return grad-like vector g_i = factor * sum_k p_k a_{k-i}, valid ranges only.
    p has length 2n-1, a has length n.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    total = 3 * n - 2
    if p.size != 2 * n - 1:
        p = np.resize(p, 2 * n - 1)
    N = next_pow2(total)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    Pr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * Pr, N)[:total]
    out = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    return factor * out


def proxy_max(c, mask=None):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    if mask is None:
        vals = c
    else:
        vals = c[mask]
    if vals.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    vals = vals[np.isfinite(vals)]
    if vals.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(vals))


def active_top(c, mask, max_k):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    size = c.size
    max_k = int(min(max_k, size))
    if max_k <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    if mask is None:
        return np.argsort(c)[::-1][:max_k]
    vals = c.copy()
    vals[~mask] = -np.inf
    return np.argsort(vals)[::-1][:max_k]


def make_top_mask(a, top_count, base_mask):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    size = 2 * n - 1
    top_count = int(top_count)
    if top_count <= 0:
        return base_mask
    if top_count >= size:
        return base_mask
    c = fft_conv(a)
    top = active_top(c, base_mask, top_count)
    if top.size == 0:
        return base_mask
    m = np.zeros(size, dtype=bool)
    m[top] = True
    if base_mask is not None:
        m[~base_mask] = False
    if not np.any(m):
        return base_mask
    return m


def safe_eval(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            return float("inf")
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            return float("inf")
        lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
        val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        if val is None:
            return float("inf")
        val = float(val)
        if not math.isfinite(val):
            return float("inf")
        return val
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def try_update(best_seq, best_score, a, label, t_global):
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        np.maximum(arr, 0.0, out=arr)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if arr.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
            return best_seq, best_score
        arr = arr / s
        sc = safe_eval(arr)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_seq = arr.copy()
            best_score = float(sc)
            print("  NEW BEST [" + label + "]: " + repr(sc) +
                  " n=" + str(arr.size) +
                  " t=" + repr(time.monotonic() - t_global))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return best_seq, best_score


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, int(new_n)), dtype=np.float64)
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if a.size == new_n:
        return norm_sum1(a)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    y = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    np.maximum(y, 0.0, out=y)
    return norm_sum1(y)


def choose_mask(a, actual_score):
    """
    Infer which subset of convolution coefficients is being maximized by the
    evaluator, mostly to make internal proxies safer. The likely actual metric
    is the full convolution maximum.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or not math.isfinite(actual_score):
        return None
    c = fft_conv(a)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    full_score = 2.0 * n * proxy_max(c, None)
    best_mask = None
    best_diff = abs(full_score - actual_score)

    masks = []
    if n > 1:
        m = np.ones(size, dtype=bool)
        nc = m.copy()
        nc[n - 1] = False
        masks.append(nc)

        ne = m.copy()
        ne[0] = False
        ne[-1] = False
        masks.append(ne)

        nce = nc.copy()
        nce[0] = False
        nce[-1] = False
        masks.append(nce)

        left = np.zeros(size, dtype=bool)
        left[:n] = True
        masks.append(left)

        right = np.zeros(size, dtype=bool)
        right[n:] = True
        masks.append(right)

    for mm in masks:
        if mm is None or not np.any(mm):
            continue
        sc = 2.0 * n * proxy_max(c, mm)
        df = abs(sc - actual_score)
        if df < best_diff:
            best_diff = df
            best_mask = mm

    # If no alternative is materially closer, use full.
    if best_mask is not None and best_diff > 0.25 * max(1.0, abs(actual_score)):
        return None
    return best_mask


def lse_a(a, beta, mask=None, reg_a=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    total = 3 * n - 2
    N = next_pow2(total)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:size]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c) & (c > 0.0), c, 0.0)

    if mask is None:
        cv = c
    else:
        cv = c[mask]

    if cv.size == 0:
        f = 0.0
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        m = float(np.max(cv))
        shifted = beta * (cv - m)
        shifted = np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 700.0)
        e = np.exp(shifted)
        se = float(e.sum())
        if se <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(se):
            e = np.zeros(cv.size, dtype=np.float64)
            e[0] = 1.0
            se = 1.0
        p = e / se

        pf = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
        if mask is None:
            pf[:] = p
        else:
            pf[mask] = p

        Pr = np.fft.rfft(pf[::-1], N)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(A * Pr, N)[:total]
        grad = 2.0 * conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
        f = m + math.log(se) / beta

    if reg_a > 0.0:
        f += 0.5 * reg_a * float(np.dot(a, a))
        grad = grad + reg_a * a

    if not math.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e100
    return f, grad


def softmax_from_u(u):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    n = u.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    um = float(np.max(u))
    e = np.exp(u - um)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / s


def make_v(a, ref=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64), 0
    if ref is None:
        if np.any(a > 0.0):
            ref = int(np.argmax(a))
        else:
            ref = 0
    ref = int(ref)
    a_ref = max(float(a[ref]), 1e-300)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)) - math.log(a_ref)
    if ref == 0:
        v = loga[1:]
    elif ref == n - 1:
        v = loga[:-1]
    else:
        v = np.concatenate((loga[:ref], loga[ref + 1:]))
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, -700.0)
    return v, ref


def from_v(v, ref):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size + 1
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ref = int(ref)
    if not (0 <= ref < n):
        ref = n - 1
    if ref > 0:
        u[:ref] = v[:ref]
    if ref < n - 1:
        u[ref + 1:] = v[ref:]
    return softmax_from_u(u)


def objgrad_v(v, beta, mask=None, reg_a=0.0, reg_u=0.0, ref=0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size + 1
    if n <= 0:
        return 1e100, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(v.size, dtype=np.float64)

    ref = int(ref)
    if not (0 <= ref < n):
        ref = n - 1

    u = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    u[ref] = 0.0
    if ref > 0:
        u[:ref] = v[:ref]
    if ref < n - 1:
        u[ref + 1:] = v[ref:]

    a = softmax_from_u(u)
    f, ga = lse_a(a, beta, mask, reg_a)
    if not math.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e100
        ga = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    mean = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gu = a * (ga - mean)

    gv = np.empty(v.size, dtype=np.float64)
    if ref > 0:
        gv[:ref] = gu[:ref]
    if ref < n - 1:
        gv[ref:] = gu[ref + 1:]

    if reg_u > 0.0:
        f += 0.5 * reg_u * float(np.dot(v, v))
        gv = gv + reg_u * v

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gv)):
        gv = np.zeros(v.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return f, gv


def estimate_lbfegs_cost(a, mask=None, beta=30.0):
    if not SCIPY_OK:
        return 0.25
    v, ref = make_v(a)
    try:
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        objgrad_v(v, beta, mask, 0.0, 0.0, ref)
        dt = time.monotonic() - t0
        if dt <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(dt):
            dt = 0.05
        return float(dt)
    except Exception:
        return 0.25


def run_lbfegs(a0, beta, mask, maxfun=500, reg_a=0.0, reg_u=1e-12, verbose=True):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a, np.array([], dtype=np.float64), 0

    v0, ref = make_v(a)
    if not SCIPY_OK or maxfun <= 0:
        return a, v0, ref

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    options = {
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxiter": int(maxfun + 30),
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-7,
        "disp": False
    }
    res = None
    try:
        res = scipy.optimize.minimize(
            objgrad_v,
            v0,
            args=(beta, mask, reg_a, reg_u, ref),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options=options
        )
    except Exception as e:
        print("    L-BFGS exception: " + repr(e))
        res = None

    if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
        vx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if vx.size == v0.size and np.all(np.isfinite(vx)):
            a_out = from_v(vx, ref)
            a_out = norm_sum1(a_out)
            v_out, ref_out = make_v(a_out)
            if verbose:
                print("    L-BFGS beta=" + repr(beta) +
                      " n=" + str(n) +
                      " eval<= " + str(int(maxfun)) +
                      " time=" + repr(time.monotonic() - t0) +
                      " success=" + str(getattr(res, "success", False)))
            return a_out, v_out, ref_out

    if verbose:
        print("    L-BFGS beta=" + repr(beta) +
              " n=" + str(n) +
              " time=" + repr(time.monotonic() - t0) +
              " (fallback)")
    return a, v0, ref


def lbfegs_phase(a_init, base_mask, budget, cost):
    a = norm_sum1(a_init)
    n = a.size
    size = 2 * n - 1
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = proxy_max(fft_conv(a), base_mask)
    current_a = a.copy()

    t1 = int(min(1000, max(50, size // 10)))
    t2 = int(min(600, max(40, size // 20)))
    t3 = int(min(400, max(30, size // 30)))
    t4 = int(max(12, size // 100))
    t5 = int(max(8, size // 160))

    schedules = [
        (10.0, None),
        (30.0, None),
        (80.0, t1),
        (120.0, t2),
        (250.0, t3),
        (600.0, t4),
        (1000.0, t5)
    ]

    total_eval_budget = int(budget / max(2.5 * cost, 0.03))
    per_run = int(max(80, min(700, max(1, total_eval_budget // max(1, len(schedules))))))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    for beta, top_count in schedules:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > budget - 5:
            break
        if top_count is None:
            msk = None
        else:
            msk = make_top_mask(current_a, top_count, base_mask)

        a_new, v_new, ref_new = run_lbfegs(
            current_a,
            beta,
            msk,
            maxfun=per_run,
            reg_a=0.0,
            reg_u=1e-12,
            verbose=True
        )
        c_new = fft_conv(a_new)
        m_new = proxy_max(c_new, base_mask)

        if math.isfinite(m_new):
            # Allow small temporary increases to explore basins, but not huge excursions.
            if m_new <= best_M * (1.0 + 1e-4) + 1e-18:
                current_a = a_new
        else:
            current_a = best_a

        if math.isfinite(m_new) and m_new < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = m_new
            best_a = a_new.copy()

    return best_a


def line_search_mix(a, g, cf, cg, fg, mask, current_M, t):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size

    base_alphas = [
        max(0.01, 1.0 / (t + 1.0)),
        max(0.02, 2.0 / (t + 2.0)),
        0.04, 0.08, 0.15, 0.25, 0.40, 0.60, 0.80, 1.0
    ]
    alphas = []
    seen = set()
    for al in base_alphas:
        al = float(al)
        if not math.isfinite(al) or al <= 0.0 or al > 1.0:
            continue
        key = int(round(al * 1000.0))
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        alphas.append(al)
    if len(alphas) == 0:
        alphas = [0.1]

    best_alpha = 0.0
    best_m = current_M
    for alpha in alphas:
        one = 1.0 - alpha
        val = (one * one) * cf + (2.0 * alpha * one) * fg + (alpha * alpha) * cg
        if mask is None:
            m = float(np.max(val))
        else:
            m = float(np.max(val[mask]))
        if math.isfinite(m) and m < best_m:
            best_m = m
            best_alpha = alpha

    if best_alpha <= 0.0:
        return a.copy(), best_m, 0.0

    new_a = (1.0 - best_alpha) * a + best_alpha * g
    new_a = np.maximum(new_a, 0.0)
    s = float(new_a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return a.copy(), best_m, 0.0
    new_a = new_a / s
    return new_a, best_m, best_alpha


def game_direction(f, active_idx, mw_iters=25, seed=0, init_logits=None):
    f = norm_sum1(f)
    n = f.size
    size = 2 * n - 1
    active_idx = np.asarray(active_idx, dtype=int)
    K = active_idx.size
    if K <= 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if init_logits is None:
        logits = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        logits = np.asarray(init_logits, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if logits.size != K:
            logits = np.tile(logits[:K] if logits.size > 0 else np.zeros(K), (max(1, K // max(1, logits.size)),))[:K]
        logits = np.where(np.isfinite(logits), logits, 0.0)
        logmx = float(np.max(logits))
        if math.isfinite(logmx):
            logits = logits - logmx
    logits += rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, K)

    def normalize_logits_to_lambda(loc):
        loc = np.where(np.isfinite(loc), loc, -1000.0)
        loc = loc - float(np.max(loc))
        w = np.exp(loc)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(K)
        return w / s

    lambda_w = normalize_logits_to_lambda(logits)
    g = None
    for _ in range(max(1, int(mw_iters))):
        pf = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
        pf[active_idx] = lambda_w

        pressure = cross_convolve_p_a(pf, f, 1.0)
        pressure = np.where(np.isfinite(pressure) & (pressure > 0.0), pressure, 0.0)

        pmx = float(np.max(pressure))
        pmn = float(np.min(pressure))
        ptp = pmx - pmn
        if ptp <= 1e-30:
            g = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        else:
            scaled = (pressure - pmn) / ptp
            w = np.exp(-8.0 * scaled)
            w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
            w += 1e-12
            g = w / float(w.sum())
            # small diffuse component avoids overly brittle directions
            g = 0.97 * g + 0.03 * (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
            g = g / float(g.sum())

        h = fft_conv2(f, g)
        ha = h[active_idx]
        ha = np.where(np.isfinite(ha), ha, 0.0)
        hmac_ = float(np.max(ha))
        hmin = float(np.min(ha))
        hptp = hmac_ - hmin
        if hptp > 1e-30:
            eta = 12.0 / hptp
            logits += eta * (ha - float(np.mean(ha)))
        lambda_w = normalize_logits_to_lambda(logits)

    return g


def lp_direction_hihts(f, active_idx, time_limit=8.0, max_k=1000):
    if not SCIPY_OK:
        return None
    if not hihts_available():
        return None

    f = norm_sum1(f)
    n = f.size
    active_idx = np.asarray(active_idx, dtype=int)
    if active_idx.size == 0:
        return None
    if active_idx.size > max_k:
        active_idx = active_idx[:max_k]
    K = active_idx.size

    row_parts = []
    col_parts = []
    val_parts = []
    for r, k in enumerate(active_idx):
        s = max(0, int(k) - n + 1)
        e = min(n, int(k) + 1)
        if s >= e:
            continue
        cols = np.arange(s, e, dtype=np.int32)
        row_parts.append(np.full(cols.size, r, dtype=np.int32))
        col_parts.append(cols)
        val_parts.append(f[cols.shape[0] + s - 1 - cols[::1]].copy() if False else f[int(k) - cols].astype(np.float64))

    if not row_parts:
        return None

    rows = np.concatenate(row_parts)
    cols = np.concatenate(col_parts)
    vals = np.concatenate(val_parts)
    A = scipy.sparse.csr_matrix((vals, (rows, cols)), shape=(K, n))

    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj[n] = 1.0

    neg_one = scipy.sparse.csc_matrix(-np.ones((K, 1)), dtype=np.float64)
    A_ub = scipy.sparse.hstack((A, neg_one), format="csr")
    A_eq = np.ones((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq[0, n] = 0.0
    b_eq = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)

    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(None, None)]

    try:
        res = scipy.optimize.linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="HiGHS",
            options={"disp": False, "time_limit": float(time_limit)}
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None

    g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g) & (g > 0.0), g, 0.0)
    gs = float(g.sum())
    if gs < 1e-15:
        return None
    return g / gs


def frank_wolfe_search(a0, budget, mask, active_max=2500, game_iters=22,
                       exact_every=7, exact_time=8.0, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_limit = max(1.0, float(budget))
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    c = fft_conv(a)
    M = proxy_max(c, mask)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    active = active_top(c, mask, active_max)

    no_improve = 0
    t = 0
    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit - 5 and no_improve < 90:
        t += 1
        time_left = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if time_left < 25:
            break

        g = None
        # Exact LP direction occasionally, only when active set is manageable.
        if hihts_available() and exact_every > 0 and (t % exact_every == 0) and time_left > 100:
            g = lp_direction_hihts(a, active, exact_time, max_k=1000)

        if g is None:
            init_log = None
            if active.size > 0:
                av = c[active]
                av = np.where(np.isfinite(av) & (av > 0.0), av, 1e-300)
                init_log = np.log(av)
                init_log -= float(np.max(init_log))
            g = game_direction(a, active, mw_iters=game_iters, seed=1000 + t, init_logits=init_log)

        if g is None:
            break

        cg = fft_conv(g)
        fg = fft_conv2(a, g)
        new_a, new_M, alpha = line_search_mix(a, g, c, cg, fg, mask, M, t)

        if new_M < M - 1e-16:
            if alpha > 0:
                one = 1.0 - alpha
                c = (one * one) * c + (2.0 * alpha * one) * fg + (alpha * alpha) * cg
                c = np.where(np.isfinite(c) & (c > 0.0), c, 0.0)
            a = new_a
            M = new_M
            no_improve = 0
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M
                best_a = a.copy()
            active = active_top(c, mask, active_max)
            if verbose and (t == 1 or t % 10 == 0):
                print("    FW t=" + str(t) +
                      " score_proxy=" + repr(2.0 * n * best_M) +
                      " alpha=" + repr(alpha) +
                      " active=" + str(active.size))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 15 == 0:
                rng = np.random.default_rng(9973 + t)
                pert = rng.normal(0.0, 0.015, n) * best_a
                a = best_a + pert
                a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
                a = a / float(a.sum())
                c = fft_conv(a)
                M = proxy_max(c, mask)
                active = active_top(c, mask, active_max)
                no_improve = 0
            if verbose and (t == 1 or t % 15 == 0):
                print("    FW stall t=" + str(t) +
                      " best_score_proxy=" + repr(2.0 * n * best_M))

    return best_a


def apply_single_move(c, a, s, d, tmove):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    c_new = c.copy()
    c_new[d:d + n] += 2.0 * tmove * a
    c_new[s:s + n] -= 2.0 * tmove * a

    t2 = tmove * tmove
    if 2 * d < c_new.size:
        c_new[2 * d] += t2
    if 2 * s < c_new.size:
        c_new[2 * s] += t2
    if s + d < c_new.size:
        c_new[s + d] -= 2.0 * t2
    c_new = np.where(np.isfinite(c_new), c_new, 0.0)
    return c_new


def random_transfer_improve(a, c, M, mask, count, rng):
    n = a.size
    improved = False
    for _ in range(int(count)):
        if int(a.size) <= 1:
            break
        s = int(rng.integers(n))
        d = int(rng.integers(n))
        if s == d or a[s] < 1e-12:
            continue
        frac = float(rng.uniform(0.002, 0.05))
        tmove = a[s] * frac
        tmove = min(tmove, a[s] * 0.99)
        if tmove <= 1e-16:
            continue
        c_new = apply_single_move(c, a, s, d, tmove)
        m_new = proxy_max(c_new, mask)
        if m_new < M - 1e-17:
            a[s] -= tmove
            a[d] += tmove
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            ss = float(a.sum())
            if ss < 1e-15:
                break
            a = a / ss
            c = fft_conv(a)
            M = proxy_max(c, mask)
            improved = True
            break
    return a, c, M, improved


def coordinate_polish(a0, budget, mask, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_limit = max(1.0, float(budget))
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    c = fft_conv(a)
    M = proxy_max(c, mask)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    n = a.size
    stall = 0
    rnd = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(777)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit - 3 and stall < 120:
        rnd += 1
        if n <= 1:
            break

        top_k = min(40, 2 * n - 1)
        if top_k <= 0:
            break
        top_idx = active_top(c, mask, top_k)
        if top_idx.size == 0:
            break

        p_top = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        p_top[top_idx] = 1.0 / float(top_idx.size)
        pressure = cross_convolve_p_a(p_top, a, 2.0)
        pressure = np.where(np.isfinite(pressure) & (pressure > 0.0), pressure, 0.0)

        src_count = min(8, n)
        dst_count = min(12, n)

        src_score = pressure * (1.0 + float(n) * a)
        src_candidates = np.argpartition(src_score, min(src_count - 1, n - 1))[:src_count]
        dst_score = pressure + 1e-5 * a
        dst_candidates = np.argpartition(dst_score, min(dst_count - 1, n - 1))[:dst_count]

        improved_any = False
        best_cand = None

        for srr in src_candidates:
            s = int(srr)
            if a[s] < 1e-12:
                continue
            for drr in dst_candidates:
                d = int(drr)
                if d == s:
                    continue
                for frac in (0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05):
                    tmove = a[s] * frac
                    if tmove <= 1e-16:
                        continue
                    tmove = min(tmove, a[s] * 0.99)
                    c_new = apply_single_move(c, a, s, d, tmove)
                    m_new = proxy_max(c_new, mask)
                    if m_new < M - 1e-18:
                        cand = (m_new, s, d, tmove)
                        if best_cand is None or cand[0] < best_cand[0]:
                            best_cand = cand
                            improved_any = True

        if not improved_any:
            # A few exploratory small transfers.
            for _ in range(25):
                s = int(rng.integers(n))
                d = int(rng.integers(n))
                if s == d or a[s] < 1e-12:
                    continue
                frac = float(rng.uniform(0.002, 0.03))
                tmove = a[s] * frac
                tmove = min(tmove, a[s] * 0.99)
                if tmove <= 1e-16:
                    continue
                c_new = apply_single_move(c, a, s, d, tmove)
                m_new = proxy_max(c_new, mask)
                if m_new < M - 1e-18:
                    cand = (m_new, s, d, tmove)
                    if best_cand is None or cand[0] < best_cand[0]:
                        best_cand = cand
                        improved_any = True

        if best_cand is not None:
            m_new, s, d, tmove = best_cand
            a[s] -= tmove
            a[d] += tmove
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            ss = float(a.sum())
            if ss < 1e-15:
                break
            a = a / ss
            c = fft_conv(a)
            M = proxy_max(c, mask)
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M
                best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0
            if verbose and (rnd <= 3 or rnd % 25 == 0):
                print("    Coord rnd=" + str(rnd) +
                      " score_proxy=" + repr(2.0 * n * best_M) +
                      " move s=" + str(s) + " d=" + str(d))
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 12 == 11:
                a, c, M, did = random_transfer_improve(a, c, M, mask, 120, rng)
                if did:
                    stall = 0
                    if M < best_M:
                        best_M = M
                        best_a = a.copy()
                else:
                    stall += 7

    return best_a


def default_sequence(n=5000):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    return norm_sum1(np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2)


def sync_work(best_seq, work_seq):
    if len(best_seq) <= 9000:
        return best_seq.copy()
    return interpolate_sequence(best_seq, 8000)


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget = max(60.0, min(float(budget_s) - 75.0, 1725.0))
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 1725.0

    print("始め: budget=" + repr(total_budget))

    try:
        parent_list = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        parent_list = None

    seq_best = None
    if parent_list is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(parent_list, dtype=np.float64)
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0, out=p)
            if p.ndim != 1:
                p = p.ravel()
            if p.size > 0 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-10:
                seq_best = norm_sum1(p)
        except Exception:
            seq_best = None

    if seq_best is None:
        seq_best = default_sequence(5000)

    best_score = safe_eval(seq_best)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = 2.0 * seq_best.size * proxy_max(fft_conv(seq_best), None)

    print("Parent/work start: n=" + str(seq_best.size) + " score=" + repr(best_score))

    # Work vector may be compressed if the parent is extremely long.
    if seq_best.size > 9000:
        work_seq = interpolate_sequence(seq_best, 8000)
    else:
        work_seq = seq_best.copy()

    # If the compressed work vector is already better (unlikely but possible), accept it.
    seq_best, best_score = try_update(seq_best, best_score, work_seq, "initial", t_start)
    work_seq = sync_work(seq_best, work_seq)

    work_score = safe_eval(work_seq)
    if not math.isfinite(work_score):
        work_score = 2.0 * work_seq.size * proxy_max(fft_conv(work_seq), None)
    mask = choose_mask(work_seq, work_score)

    lb_cost = estimate_lbfegs_cost(work_seq, None, beta=30.0)
    print("L-BFGS cost estimate: " + repr(lb_cost))

    def refresh_work_from_best():
        nonlocal work_seq, mask
        nw = sync_work(seq_best, work_seq)
        if nw.size != work_seq.size:
            sc = safe_eval(nw)
            if not math.isfinite(sc):
                sc = 2.0 * nw.size * proxy_max(fft_conv(nw), None)
            mask = choose_mask(nw, sc)
        work_seq = nw

    refresh_work_from_best()

    # Phase 1: L-BFGS smoothed active-set/global optimization.
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_left = total_budget - (t0 - t_start)
    if SCIPY_OK and time_left > 350:
        phase_budget = min(time_left * 0.46, 820.0)
        print("\n=== Phase 1: L-BFGS (budget=" + repr(phase_budget) + "s) ===")
        a_lb = lbfegs_phase(work_seq, mask, phase_budget, lb_cost)
        seq_best, best_score = try_update(seq_best, best_score, a_lb, "lbfegs", t_start)
        refresh_work_from_best()

    # Phase 2: Frank-Wolfe search.
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_left = total_budget - (t0 - t_start)
    if time_left > 260:
        phase_budget = min(time_left * 0.31, 520.0)
        print("\n=== Phase 2: Frank-Wolfe (budget=" + repr(phase_budget) + "s) ===")
        a_fw = frank_wolfe_search(work_seq, phase_budget, mask,
                                  active_max=2600,
                                  game_iters=24,
                                  exact_every=7,
                                  exact_time=7.0,
                                  verbose=True)
        seq_best, best_score = try_update(seq_best, best_score, a_fw, "fw", t_start)
        refresh_work_from_best()

    # Phase 3: Exact coordinate polish.
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_left = total_budget - (t0 - t_start)
    if time_left > 160:
        phase_budget = min(time_left * 0.20, 320.0)
        print("\n=== Phase 3: Coordinate polish (budget=" + repr(phase_budget) + "s) ===")
        a_cp = coordinate_polish(work_seq, phase_budget, mask, verbose=True)
        seq_best, best_score = try_update(seq_best, best_score, a_cp, "coord", t_start)
        refresh_work_from_best()

    # Phase 4: Length probes, only if the inferred metric is the full maximum.
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_left = total_budget - (t0 - t_start)
    if time_left > 360 and mask is None:
        print("\n=== Phase 4: Length probes ===")
        base_len = seq_best.size if seq_best.size <= 9000 else work_seq.size
        candidates = [base_len + 250, base_len - 250, base_len + 550, base_len - 550]
        tried = set()
        for target in candidates:
            time_left = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
            if time_left < 180:
                break
            target = int(max(1200, min(9000, target)))
            if target in tried:
                continue
            tried.add(target)
            print("  Trying n=" + str(target))
            if seq_best.size <= 9000:
                a0 = interpolate_sequence(seq_best, target)
            else:
                a0 = interpolate_sequence(work_seq, target)

            if SCIPY_OK and time_left > 230:
                per = int(max(80, min(260, int(time_left / max(4.0 * lb_cost, 0.1)))))
                a_new, _, _ = run_lbfegs(a0, 90.0, None, maxfun=per, verbose=False)
                seq_best, best_score = try_update(seq_best, best_score, a_new, "len_lb_" + str(target), t_start)
                refresh_work_from_best()

            time_left = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
            if time_left > 110:
                if seq_best.size == target:
                    a_base = seq_best.copy()
                else:
                    a_base = a0
                a_fw2 = frank_wolfe_search(a_base, min(time_left * 0.45, 90.0), None,
                                           active_max=1600,
                                           game_iters=16,
                                           exact_every=5,
                                           exact_time=5.0,
                                           verbose=False)
                seq_best, best_score = try_update(seq_best, best_score, a_fw2, "len_fw_" + str(target), t_start)
                refresh_work_from_best()

    # Phase 5: Final local polish on whatever is best.
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_left = total_budget - (t0 - t_start)
    if time_left > 120:
        phase_budget = min(time_left * 0.35, 180.0)
        print("\n=== Phase 5: Final coordinate (budget=" + repr(phase_budget) + "s) ===")
        a_cp2 = coordinate_polish(work_seq, phase_budget, mask, verbose=False)
        seq_best, best_score = try_update(seq_best, best_score, a_cp2, "final_coord", t_start)
        refresh_work_from_best()

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_left = total_budget - (t0 - t_start)
    if SCIPY_OK and time_left > 80:
        phase_budget = min(time_left * 0.40, 100.0)
        top_count = int(min(500, max(50, (2 * work_seq.size - 1) // 20)))
        msk = make_top_mask(work_seq, top_count, mask)
        per = int(max(60, min(220, int(phase_budget / max(3.0 * lb_cost, 0.1)))))
        print("\n=== Phase 6: Final L-BFGS active polish ===")
        a_lb2, _, _ = run_lbfegs(work_seq, 250.0, msk, maxfun=per, verbose=False)
        seq_best, best_score = try_update(seq_best, best_score, a_lb2, "final_lb", t_start)
        refresh_work_from_best()

    # Final validation and return.
    final_val = safe_eval(seq_best)
    if math.isfinite(final_val):
        if final_val < best_score:
            best_score = final_val
    else:
        # Should not happen, but keep a valid fallback.
        seq_best = default_sequence(5000)
        best_score = safe_eval(seq_best)

    arr = np.asarray(seq_best, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if arr.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
        arr = default_sequence(5000)
    else:
        arr = arr / s

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print("\nFINAL: score=" + repr(best_score) +
          " n=" + str(arr.size) +
          " elapsed=" + repr(elapsed))

    return [float(x) for x in arr]
```
